# Compact launch against the full grid: 16x64x128

Kernel BLOCK 16x64x128, 1 waves, 1024 programs. 3 launches per mode plus 2 recorded. grid: full / compact; working: programs with stream-K work in the compact launch; C: bit-identical (or both correct, for atomic_iz and bf16 partials); XCC rotations r (workgroup p on XCC (p + r) % 8); CUs: the fewest distinct CUs of an XCD over its working programs, compact launch; at most per CU.
